### [1] [Warm-Start Quantum Computation for Energy Readout and State Preparation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12485)
*Tien D. Kieu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了WARP框架,将量子基态能量估计和制备问题分解为前端(提供近似态)和后端(读取或精炼)两个模块,核心发现是两者之间唯一传递的信息是谱权重分布p_n=|⟨E_n|χ⟩|²。通过七次前端替换验证了模块化设计,并展示了四种后端方法:信号处理读出、绝热演化、离散放大和递归方法。在拉伸N₂分子上,该方法将能量误差从79 mHa降低到1.4×10⁻⁶ Ha,达到化学精度要求,并能测量低于散粒噪声基底四个数量级的微弱重叠度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子算法将基态制备的前端(变分态准备)和后端(能量提取/态精炼)紧密耦合,导致算法设计缺乏灵活性。作者发现谱权重分布p_n是连接两个模块的唯一关键信息,这意味着可以将前端设计为可互换模块。此外,变分方法虽能制备近似基态,但其能量估计受变分上界限制,常常远离化学精度要求。因此需要开发新的后端方法来充分利用已制备态的谱信息,在不重新设计前端的情况下大幅提升精度。

Method: 提出WARP(谱权重驱动的模块化)框架,核心思想是用谱权重分布p_n=|⟨E_n|χ⟩|²作为前后端的唯一接口。通过七次前端替换实验验证模块独立性。设计四种后端策略:(A)读出方法:在量子设备上采样时间演化信号g(t)=⟨χ|e^(-iH_Pt)|χ⟩,用经典信号处理提取真实本征值,所需信号长度仅为傅里叶变换的1/50;(B)绝热演化:最小能隙由重叠度决定;(C)离散放大:深度更浅但需要测量值而非上界;(D)深度为O(3^m)的递归方法。对于微弱重叠度测量,开发了差拍技术,将强参考态与弱参考态对比,突破散粒噪声极限四个数量级。

Result: 在拉伸N₂分子系统上的实验显示:(1)基于采样的对角化方法给出的变分能量上界比真实基态能高79 mHa,误差是化学精度(1.6 mHa)的50倍;(2)使用读出后端处理同一个已制备态,无需新前端或新量子电路,将基态能量精度提升至1.4×10⁻⁶ Ha,比化学精度高三个数量级;(3)差拍测量技术成功检测到比散粒噪声基底低四个数量级的重叠度;(4)通过七次前端模块替换验证了框架的模块化特性;(5)系统绘制了各方法的操作范围图并明确了各自的局限性。实验证明谱权重分布确实是定价所有后端性能的关键量。

Conclusion: WARP框架成功将量子基态问题解耦为以谱权重分布为接口的模块化系统,证明了前端可作为可互换模块而不影响后端性能。核心发现是谱权重分布p_n包含了所有下游任务所需的定价信息。基于信号处理的读出后端能够突破变分上界限制,在不增加量子资源的情况下将能量精度提升三个数量级至化学精度以内。微弱重叠度的精确测量(突破散粒噪声限制四个数量级)为评估和优化算法性能提供了关键工具。该框架为量子算法设计提供了新的模块化思路,使得前端优化和后端方法可以独立发展,并为实用量子化学计算开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Estimating a ground-state energy and preparing a ground state both split into a front-end that supplies an approximate state $|χ\rangle$ and a back-end that reads or refines it. We argue, and measure at every step, that the only thing crossing that split is the front-end's spectral weight distribution $p_n=|\langle E_n|χ\rangle|^2$ over the eigenstates of the problem Hamiltonian $H_P$. Everything downstream is priced in it, so the front-end is a swappable module, and we swap it seven times without altering anything to its right. We call the arrangement WARP.
  Four back-ends spend that distribution. A readout (A) samples $g(t)=\langleχ|e^{-iH_Pt}|χ\rangle$ on the device and extracts true eigenvalues by classical signal processing, returning an energy below the variational bound of the state it was handed, from a signal $50\times$ shorter than a Fourier transform needs. Three routes spend the same account on a state instead. An adiabatic schedule (B) has a minimum gap set by the overlap; a discrete amplification (C) is cheaper in depth but must halt at an index the overlap fixes, so it needs a measured value and not a bound; a recursion (D) of depth $\mathcal{O}(3^m)$ raises the question of whether it can be run at all.
  The interactions are the substance. On stretched N$_2$ a sample-based diagonalisation reports a variational bound 79 mHa high, fifty times outside chemical accuracy; reading the state it has already prepared returns $E_0$ to $1.4\times10^{-6}$ Ha, three orders of magnitude inside it, and needs no new front-end and no new circuit. And because the weights price everything, they must be measurable when scarce, so we beat a weak reference against a strong one and recover overlaps four orders of magnitude below the shot-noise floor. We map the operating envelope throughout, and state the limitations.

</details>


### [2] [Exact solutions of non-Markovian dissipative Ising models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12546)
*Mohammad Maghrebi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了同时具有非马尔可夫动力学和马尔可夫耗散的多体量子系统，通过精确随机表示方法将自旋-玻色子耦合系统解耦为单个自旋演化，实现了精确求解，并揭示了两种动力学相互作用对自旋压缩等量子效应的重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子器件和模拟器的应用需要准确理解其动力学行为和环境噪声效应。然而，当系统与谐振腔或振动模式耦合时会产生非马尔可夫动力学，加上马尔可夫耗散的存在，使得相互作用多体系统的表征变得非常困难。现有方法难以在不进行绝热消除玻色子的情况下精确描述这类复杂系统的动力学演化。

Method: 本文采用精确随机表示（exact stochastic representation）方法来处理问题。研究对象是自旋通过有损玻色模式耦合并介导伊辛型相互作用的原型模型，排除横向磁场但允许与相互作用不对易的局域自旋耗散（包括退相、弛豫、泵浦以及产生相干性的过程）。该方法将多体动力学解耦为单个自旋的演化，从而在不绝热消除玻色子的前提下获得精确的自旋可观测量和关联函数。

Result: 研究确定了一大类可精确求解的、同时具有非马尔可夫动力学和马尔可夫耗散的多体量子系统。在马尔可夫极限下，得到的解描述了具有关联退相和非互易相互作用的伊辛模型。以自旋压缩为例的分析表明，可实现的压缩程度及其最优制备时间强烈依赖于非马尔可夫动力学和马尔可夫耗散之间的相互作用。

Conclusion: 本文为同时包含非马尔可夫动力学和马尔可夫耗散的相互作用多体量子系统提供了精确求解方法，无需绝热消除玻色子即可获得精确解。研究揭示了两种动力学机制的协同效应对量子系统性能（如自旋压缩）具有决定性影响，为准确理解和优化量子器件及模拟器在复杂噪声环境下的行为提供了重要的理论工具。

Abstract: Applications of quantum devices and simulators require an accurate understanding of their dynamics as well as the effects of environmental noise. While some sources of noise can be modeled as Markovian dissipation, coupling to resonators or vibrational modes often produces non-Markovian dynamics as well, making it difficult to characterize the system, particularly for interacting many-body systems. Here, we identify a broad class of exactly solvable many-body quantum systems with both non-Markovian dynamics and Markovian dissipation. We consider a prototypical model of spins coupled to lossy bosonic modes that mediate Ising-type interactions. We exclude a transverse field but allow local spin dissipation that need not commute with the interactions, including dephasing, relaxation, pumping, and even processes that generate coherences. Using an exact stochastic representation, we decouple the many-body dynamics into the evolution of individual spins, yielding exact spin observables and correlations without adiabatically eliminating the bosons. In the Markovian limit, our solutions describe Ising models with correlated dephasing and nonreciprocal interactions. An example of spin squeezing shows that both the achievable squeezing and its optimal preparation time depend strongly on the interplay between non-Markovian dynamics and Markovian dissipation.

</details>


### [3] [Engineering Ergodicity-Breaking and Stable Emergent Conservation Laws in a Generic Quantum Many-body System Under Strong Periodic Drive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12551)
*Sagnik Chaudhuri,Anirban Das,Asmi Haldar,Arnab Das*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了强驱动量子多体系统中的动力学冻结(DF)现象,识别了遍历性破缺的条件,并提出通过精确的局域工程方法稳定DF。研究表明非DF系统的Floquet有效哈密顿量在自旋翻转序下是非局域的,而经过局域DF工程后可变为准局域,同时系统在每个时刻仍保持非可积性。这种非普适性在平衡态系统中不存在,为工程新型遍历性破缺的相互作用Floquet物质态提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期驱动量子多体系统通常遵循遍历性假设趋向局域无限温度态,但强驱动下会出现动力学冻结导致遍历性破缺。然而最近研究发现在特定设置下DF存在例外情况。因此需要明确强驱动系统中遍历性破缺的条件,并寻找稳定DF的有效方法,以克服现有理论的局限性并拓展其应用潜力。

Method: 研究者通过理论分析识别了强驱动系统中遍历性破缺的充分必要条件,并发展了精确的局域工程技术来稳定动力学冻结现象。方法的核心是通过局域调控使得Floquet有效哈密顿量在自旋翻转序下从非局域转变为准局域,同时保持系统在每个时刻的非可积性。这种工程方法简单且局域化,不破坏系统的基本相互作用特性。

Result: 研究发现非DF系统的Floquet有效哈密顿量H_eff = (i/T)ln[U(T)]在按自旋翻转排序时呈现非局域性。通过提出的局域DF工程方法,成功将有效哈密顿量转变为准局域,同时维持每个时刻哈密顿量H(t)的非可积性。这揭示了驱动量子系统中令人困惑的非普适性现象——非可积性不再普遍地意味着遍历性和热化,这与平衡态系统的行为形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 本工作为在各种量子平台上工程新型遍历性破缺的相互作用Floquet物质态开辟了道路,突破了传统遍历性假设的限制。研究成果赋予了基于动力学冻结的量子误差缓解和态稳定技术前所未有的通用性和灵活性,为量子信息处理和量子模拟提供了新的理论基础和实践工具。这种非平衡系统中非可积性与遍历性解耦的发现,挑战了传统统计物理学的普适性观念,开启了探索非平衡量子物质新奇相的新方向。

Abstract: A periodically driven quantum many-body system is believed to approach a locally infinite-temperature-like state as dictated by the ergodicity hypothesis, when all the couplings/parameters are of the same order. When the drive is stronger than a threshold value, a large class of systems is shown to break this mandate, exhibiting Dynamical Freezing (DF): ergodicity is broken due to the emergence of local operators with permanent memory of their initial values (PRX 11, 021008, 2021). However, an exception to DF has recently been noted in a particular setting (PRX 16, 011041, 2026). Here we identify the condition for ergodicity-breaking in a system under strong drive, and show how to stabilize DF by exact, local engineering. We further show that the Floquet Hamiltonian $H_{eff} = (i/T)\ln{[U(T)]}$ is non-local when ordered by spin-flips, in a non-DF system, while it turns quasi-local under our simple, local DF engineering that keeps the Hamiltonian $H(t)$ still non-integrable at each $t$. This perplexing non-universality is absent in equilibrium, where non-integrability is believed to imply ergodicity and thermalization universally. Our work paves a way to engineering novel ergodicity-broken interacting Floquet phases of matter in various quantum platforms, and also endows DF-assisted quantum error mitigation and state stabilization techniques (see Nature 653, 1027 (2026)) with unprecedented versatility and flexibility.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算机和传感器中超导电路需要集成低损耗介电材料以提升性能。现有固态量子系统存在声子介导的相关误差问题,需要开发新的材料策略来阻止非热声子在超导体-基底界面传播,从而消除这类误差并改善器件性能。

Conclusion: 本研究首次展示了利用二维材料热学特性的材料驱动策略,通过hBN薄层阻止器件基底激发的非热声子跨越超导体-基底界面传播,为消除固态量子系统中声子介导的相关误差迈出了第一步。该技术应用于量子传感器时,可通过阻止声子进入无源金属薄膜来最大化声子收集效率,具有广泛应用前景。

Abstract: The integration of low-loss dielectrics into superconducting circuits is of importance to the functionality of quantum computers and sensors. In this work, we fabricate and measure niobium resonators atop few-layer, epitaxial hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) grown by Molecular Beam Epitaxy on a sapphire substrate and compare against a control counterpart without hBN. We conduct radiofrequency measurements which probe the responses to power, temperature, and temporal fluctuations of two-level systems. The resonators integrating hBN maintain low dielectric loss with a low-power internal loss tangent on the order of $10^{-6}$, and demonstrate hBN can be added to the conventional superconducting qubit stack with minimal degradation of the quality factor. This research represents a first step towards a materials-driven strategy to eliminate phonon-mediated correlated errors in solid-state quantum systems, which leverages the thermal properties of 2D materials to prevent athermal phonons excited in the device substrate from transmitting across the superconductor-substrate interface. The same technique applied to quantum sensors would maximize phonon collection by blocking phonons from entering passive metal films.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Spin-dependent forces enable independent control over the effective frequencies of bosonic motional modes and their coupling strengths to spins in trapped-ion spin-boson quantum simulators. We show that the relative signs of these effective frequencies provide an important programmable resource for engineering distinct multimode dynamics. Focusing on a two-mode quantum Rabi model, changing the frequencies of the two modes from the same sign to opposite signs reorganizes the resonance structure of the spin-mediated bosonic interaction, converting the dominant resonant process from excitation exchange to correlated pair creation. In the dispersive regime, the resulting effective interaction realizes a two-mode parametric amplifier with stable oscillatory, critical, and exponentially amplifying regimes separated by a spin-dependent instability threshold. Across these regimes, the dynamics generate collective-quadrature squeezing and bipartite motional entanglement. Preparing the spin in a coherent superposition further allows different spin components to experience distinct stability regimes, generating tripartite spin-boson entanglement, while spin postselection can coherently combine the conditional bosonic branches to generate non-Gaussian entangled bosonic states. Finally, we outline an implementation using parameters within reach of current trapped-ion platforms.

</details>


### [6] [Error correction and quantum information spreading on sparse random graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12597)
*Giovanni Cemin,Marin Bukov,Andrea Solfanelli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了稀疏图几何结构上的量子纠错码，揭示了谱维度（spectral dimension）作为连接图结构、量子信息动力学和纠错码性能的普适参数，发现了由谱维度决定的几何诱导纠缠相变，并确定了在中等谱维度下可实现码距离的最优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错码面临高维构造的优越性能与近期硬件局域性需求之间的矛盾。稀疏中间几何结构可能结合两者优势,但其结构如何支配量子信息动力学和码性能尚不清楚。需要找到统一的参数来理解和优化稀疏图上量子纠错码的性能。

Method: 使用两种不同的可调谱维度稀疏图架构,在其上构建Floquet随机Clifford电路。通过图拉普拉斯算子的低能谱定义谱维度$d_s$。系统研究谱维度对量子信息传播、纠缠结构和量子纠错码距离标度的影响,分析不同相的性质及相变临界行为。

Result: 发现谱维度$d_s$普适地支配量子信息扩散和纠错码距离的标度律。识别出从局域面积律到遍历体积律的几何诱导纠缠相变,其普适性由$d_s$决定并表现出长程逾渗临界性。在遍历相中,电路作为CSS码编码器,其码距离由切断图的代价和最短缠绕环长度之间的竞争决定。码距离在中等谱维度$d_s$处达到最大值,同时保持稀疏连接性和物理可实现性。

We identify a geometry-induced entanglement phase transition from localized area-law to ergodic volume-law, whose universality is determined by $d_s$ and exhibits long-range percolation criticality. In the ergodic phase, the circuit acts as a CSS code encoder whose code distance is set by the competition between two logical sectors --- the cost of severing the graph and the length of its shortest winding cycle --- which connectivity pushes in opposite directions. Abstract: We study symmetries and anomalies of dissipative Majorana chains described by Lindblad dynamics. Using the operator-state mapping, we represent the Lindbladian as a non-Hermitian operator on a doubled Hilbert space. In this formulation, the ket and bra contours admit strong fermion parities and strong translations, while their diagonal combinations define weak symmetries. For even and odd chain lengths, we diagnose anomalies by comparing symmetry algebras with and without weak fermion-parity defects. These algebras retain the lattice precursor of the modulo-$8$ anomaly of the closed Majorana chain and exhibit projective relations between strong fermion parity and weak translation, as well as between weak fermion parity and strong translation, which are intrinsic to open quantum systems. We establish the IR correspondence between the lattice symmetry algebras and those of the doubled Majorana CFT in four spin sectors, identifying chiral fermion parities emanated from the lattice translations. Finally, we develop the lattice bosonization of the dissipative chain. For even length with collective energy dephasing, combining the defect and no-defect sectors and imposing GSO projections gives rise to an Ising Lindbladian with non-invertible Kramers--Wannier symmetries descending from the anomalous strong translation and fermion parity of the Majorana chain. For odd length, constructing mutually commuting ket- and bra-sector spin operators instead yields an Ising Lindbladian with a Kramers--Wannier duality defect. Our results provide a controlled framework for extending lattice anomalies and non-invertible symmetries to open quantum systems.

Abstract: We investigate the construction of unitary $t$-designs in a multi-photon Fock space on programmable linear-optical interferometers. For example, we find numerical evidence suggesting that for unitary 1-designs, the required number of programmable parameters scales empirically as $2d$ with the number of modes $d$, instead of $O(d^2)$. Furthermore, as a practical result, we develop a photon-number estimation protocol for multi-photon Fock states that leverages these optimized 1- and 2-designs. Our findings demonstrate that shallow interferometers can accurately reproduce Haar-averaged first moments, while second-moment-sensitive observables provide a considerably stricter test for approximate unitary 2-designs.

Abstract: Quantum Zeno dynamics constrains a continuously monitored system to measurement-selected subspaces. Here we show that the structure of such Zeno subspaces can be governed by a \emph{latent symmetry}, namely a symmetry that is absent from the geometry of the underlying physical network. We consider a minimal circuit-QED network of coupled microwave resonators subject to quantum-nondemolition (QND) monitoring of local photon occupations. Two distinguished resonators are geometrically inequivalent, yet are exactly exchanged by a latent symmetry of the network. Strong monitoring partitions the single-excitation Hilbert space into three quantum-Zeno subspaces, two of which correspond to localization on the distinguished resonators. Latent symmetry exactly exchanges these two Zeno subspaces and enforces identical escape and residence statistics despite their different microscopic environments. In the strong-measurement regime their residence times grow linearly with the measurement strength and diverge in the ideal Zeno limit, where the measurement subspaces become dynamically disconnected. Our results show that latent symmetry can act as a hidden organizing principle of quantum Zeno dynamics, imposing exact relations between measurement-selected subspaces that are geometrically inequivalent in the physical system.

Abstract: We construct exact deterministic circuits for transposing and inverting an unknown qutrit unitary $U\in\mathrm{SU}(3)$ using only forward queries. Three queries prepare a catalyst independently of the input state. Four further queries implement transposition while returning the catalyst unchanged and uncorrelated with the output. Two uses within an antisymmetric encoding implement inversion with eight processing queries and return the same catalyst. Reusing the catalyst implements $m$ transpositions or inversions with $3+4m$ or $3+8m$ total queries, respectively. More generally, we prove that exact transposition and inversion of an unknown $U\in\mathrm{SU}(d)$, $d\ge2$, require at least $d+1$ and $d^2-1$ processing queries, respectively, in fixed causal order. These processing bounds hold for any program state prepared independently of the input state using finitely many forward queries, even if that state is consumed during processing. Both our catalytic qutrit algorithms attain their respective bounds. Moreover, exact qutrit transposition with four processing queries requires at least three preparation queries, and our construction attains this bound.

Abstract: Quantum LDPC codes promise to reduce the overhead of fault-tolerant quantum computation, but their practical use requires efficient logical gates, measurements, and access to non-Clifford resources. We further prove that every rank one logical operator admits $D$ pairwise disjoint physical representatives, enabling parallel gauging and a space--time tradeoff for logical measurements. Finally, we construct an addressable surface code interface for injecting externally prepared resource states into selected logical qubits. Together, these results establish La-cross codes as a simple, low-connectivity generalisation of surface code computation, retaining analogous computational primitives while providing a complete Clifford+$T$ instruction set across multiple encoded qubits.

这些结果证明了CLP码在多个校验权重下能够实现更优的量子码参数。

Abstract: We introduce coset lifted product (CLP) codes, a family of quantum LDPC codes that combine the protograph structure of lifted product codes with the coset action of Aydin, Tamo, and Barg \cite{AydinTamoBarg2026}. Given a finite group $G$, a subgroup $H \le G$ that need not be normal, a protograph $A$ over $\mathbb F_2[G]$, and a protograph $B$ over $\mathbb F_2[N_G(H)]$, the two protographs act on the cosets $G/H$ from the left and from the right to produce quantum CSS codes. Further, for groups of odd order, we give an explicit formula for the dimension as a sum over the irreducible representations $ρ$ of $G$ of hypergraph product dimensions. A search over $14,843$ pairs $(G,H)$ with $H$ restricted to be non-normal and $120 \le n \le 400$ yields certified codes that exceed the best published value of $Q=kd^2/n$ at check weights $6,7$ and $9$, i.e., we obtain $[[360,20,20]]$ at $w=6$, a $[[360,24,20]]$ code at $w=7$, and codes $[[252,16,18]]$ and $[[288,16,20]]$ at $w=9$. Our search also produce high-distance codes with parameters $[[168,10,14]], [[240,8,20]]$ at $w=6$ and $[[216,8,18]]$ at $w=7$.

Abstract: Frustrated magnets host rich emergent behavior because competing interactions generate dense manifolds of low-energy states. The Ising Shastry-Sutherland model is a paradigmatic setting, motivated experimentally by Ising-like rare-earth tetraborides such as TmB$_4$ and ErB$_4$, whose field-induced magnetization plateaus have motivated extensive microscopic modeling. Yet how tunable quantum fluctuations reorganize this material-motivated phase diagram remains largely unexplored. Here, we realize a transverse- and longitudinal-field Ising model on the Shastry-Sutherland lattice using a programmable Rydberg-atom array. We map its quantum phase diagram using density matrix renormalization group calculations and probe corresponding signatures experimentally in arrays containing more than 110 atoms. Beyond the established classical phases, the calculations identify two regimes absent in the classical limit and stabilized at finite transverse field: a $2/5$ filling plateau whose finite stability region emerges from a classically degenerate phase boundary and a symmetry-preserving quantum-dimer regime replacing classically selected collinear order. Experiments on finite open arrays reveal signatures consistent with these regimes across three boundary-pinning conditions, with the resulting response maps agreeing qualitatively with the calculated phase diagram. Our results establish programmable Rydberg-atom arrays as tools for exploring frustrated, material-motivated Hamiltonians and motivate searches for new, quantum-driven magnetization plateaus and quantum-dimer behavior in ErB$_4$ under a transverse field or in strain-tuned TmB$_4$.

Abstract: We study byte-level autoregressive language models whose hidden state is a small quantum register, whose transitions are learned token-conditioned quantum channels, and whose next-byte probabilities come directly from Born-rule measurements without a learned classical vocabulary projection or softmax. In the fixed-width recurrent setting, unitary dynamics cannot contract or reset stored distinctions; experimentally, unitary recurrence loses to its retrained one-token control in every tested variant. Introducing learned dissipation, then token-conditioned dissipative gating, yields useful memory beyond one token. The gated model improves test negative log-likelihood by 0.20 +/- 0.03 nats/byte over retrained parameter-matched one-token controls at context length 12, and by 0.29-0.33 at longer contexts, but never beats retrained two-token controls. A key-value recall benchmark shows a width-dependent threshold: one stored pair meets the pre-registered accuracy bar at 4-5 memory qubits, two pairs at 6-7, and three pairs remain unsolved. This is consistent with, but does not establish, a capacity explanation for the short effective memory horizon. As a secondary result, one-qubit models reach lower stationary memory entropy than a heuristically optimized classical predictive frontier at matched error in 9/9 seeds. On IBM Heron hardware, computational-basis memory-register distributions show signal against a uniform null in 12/12 circuits across prefixes of up to 12 tokens, next-bit probabilities pass pre-registered accuracy bars at four-token prefixes, and bytes are generated autoregressively by sampling physically measured bit probabilities. The tested models are classically simulable and substantially worse than classical n-gram baselines. We claim no computational quantum advantage.

Abstract: A bipartite observable is stable when its idealized responses are continuous. We show that it is NIP exactly when they are of the first Baire class. They then form a Rosenthal compactum, and the dividing lines of Shelah in model theory, together with the classification of Rosenthal compacta of Todorčević, acquire a physical meaning. For observables, mixtures of states make every hereditarily separable compactum of idealized responses metrizable, and the Todorčević trichotomy becomes a classification into three classes. Each class contains a physical order: the comparison of photon numbers, the comparison of positions, and the causal order of spacetime, whose idealized responses are not first countable because a light cone can shrink to a point; the comparison of two phases on a circle joins it there. Each of the seven minimal families of the heptachotomy, which Argyros, Dodos and Kanellopoulos extracted from the methods of Todorčević, is realized by a tree of preparations of photons, of a particle in a box, or of a spin chain read by a probe; the spin chain also shows that NIP, unlike its uniform version, is not symmetric between the two parties. Finally, for the Ising model in a transverse field we show that, in the ordered phase, the responses of the two pure phases form split pairs whose midpoints, the responses of the symmetric state, form an uncountable discrete set; the proof uses a Lee--Yang theorem for this model.

Abstract: The Markov length, the length scale governing the exponential decay of conditional mutual information (CMI) in a quantum many-body state, has proven useful for characterizing mixed-state phases of matter. Nevertheless, we show that a state's Markov length is not generally stable under weak local channels. The failure of stability occurs already at a pure product state. We demonstrate this explicitly in a one-dimensional fermionic chain, where a fine-tuned depth-two circuit of nearest-neighbor Gaussian channels maps the vacuum to a state whose CMI decays \emph{algebraically} and whose Markov length is therefore infinite, even when the channels are taken arbitrarily close to the identity. The algebraic decay is rooted in the nonanalyticity of the von Neumann entropy at rank-deficient density operators. We exhibit an analogous instability on an ordinary qubit chain. More generally, we show that the Markov length is unstable in any translation-invariant state with even a single rank-deficient reduced density operator. These results pose a challenge to recent definitions of mixed-state phase equivalence based on the Markov length.

As an application, we apply the framework to the fiber-tapping detection problem.

Abstract: The indirect-control paradigm offers a scalable approach to quantum computation by restricting external operations to a small subset of qubits, whereas the entire system evolves under its intrinsic many-body Hamiltonian. This framework reduces hardware complexity and limits the noise introduced through control channels. Recently, Anan et al. proposed an implementation of the variational quantum eigensolver (VQE) within this indirect-control architecture. However, the impact and integration of quantum error mitigation techniques in such a setting remain largely unexplored. In particular, zero-noise extrapolation (ZNE), a widely used error mitigation strategy, has not yet been systematically adapted to indirect-control VQE. A key requirement of ZNE is the controlled amplification of noise, which is commonly achieved through circuit-level techniques such as unitary folding. In the indirect-control framework, however, quantum operations are largely governed by continuous time-evolution under the system Hamiltonian, making the construction of physically realizable inverse operations non-trivial. In this study, we investigate the feasibility of implementing ZNE within the indirect-controlVQEframework.We address the challenges associated with unitary folding in the presence of time-evolution operators and, with a minimal relaxation of the indirect-control constraint, we propose a ZNE-based error-mitigation strategy that exploits the symmetry of the one-dimensional XY spin chain.

Abstract: The resolution of spectroscopic measurements is fundamentally limited by the convolution between a system's intrinsic response and the instrument response function, creating an ill-posed inverse problem. While entangled photons can theoretically bypass these limits, low photon flux has hindered their experimental realization. Employing the signal beam as a broadband probe, QCCS achieves all-optical deconvolution through a correlation-enhanced sum-frequency generation channel between the convoluted anti-Stokes and idler beams. By subtracting the uncorrelated background using a 500-femtosecond temporal window, we successfully retrieve narrow Raman bands (8 cm^{-1} and 15 cm^{-1}) completely obscured by a 75 cm^{-1} probe bandwidth without numerical deconvolution or prior knowledge. The robust receiver architecture preserves the baseband noise-suppression advantages, inherently achieves perfect mode balancing for asymmetric optical detection losses, and fundamentally closes the image-frequency crosstalk. Practical experiments have validated the feasibility of the proposed architecture and its effective suppression of image-frequency noise, achieving a performance of 11.4 Mbps @ 42 km. Abstract: The uncontrolled endpoint channel can conceal how well a quantum process can protect a logical task under time-resolved control. For unknown-state transmission the baseline is exactly $1-d^{-2}$. Two qubit processes with the same endpoint channel have optimal errors $0$ and $p=\sin^2(2gh)$ at the same finite pulse budget in an ideal-transparent system-only window architecture, forcing any endpoint-only predictor to err by at least $p/2$ on one of them. An exactly solvable family shows how the allocation of a fixed interaction phase around a control window sets the attainable error, and a nontrivial perfect echo requires and attains total control action $π$ in that architecture. The separation extends directly to every qubit unitary target at a budget that includes its boundary gate, while unitary tasks in any dimension have baseline $1-d^{-2}$ whenever a budget-feasible free realization gives the depolarizing output. Complete-record implementation bounds quantify coherent and dissipative control imperfections. Continuous-process randomized compilation supplies physical instrument pullbacks; compatible completion conditions extend their action, and certified dynamical bounds yield achievable task-error and capability guarantees. The resulting theory identifies temporal structure and control investment that endpoint characterization alone cannot resolve.

Rounds at different p compose exactly, so every schedule of polarisations defines a composite channel whose entanglement-breaking status can be certified in ball arithmetic. A gated search of 33 million random circuits finds no valley with contrast above about 3e-3, with an interior maximum near index 90, and yields six certified targets. A 53-round pulse certifiably beats the static protocol, a depth-three valley is walked down its staircase by pulse duration, and a 1.2 per cent coherent tilt of the ancilla opens the switch at fixed population. Closing the switch costs 21 to 35 rounds. The asymmetry is not one of local rates, which agree to within about two per cent at matched circuit positions (certified). It is one of distance and response: closing must remove an entanglement margin 2.2 times larger, and while hot rounds added to a window hold act almost independently, window rounds added to a hot hold act collectively. Near the crossing, the lever of the log-margin is that of a linear approach to the entanglement-breaking boundary, with no free parameter. Abstract: Nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond are promising quantum sensors for AC magnetic fields. However, conventional dynamical-decoupling-based magnetometry becomes technically demanding at high frequencies because of the requirement for increasingly short and high-power microwave pulses. This paper describes an AC magnetometry technique based on continuous-wave optically detected magnetic resonance (CW-ODMR) measurements, which enables the detection of signals in the hundred-MHz range. By applying a strong static magnetic field transverse to the NV axis, the energy separation between the relevant electron-spin states was tuned and resonantly coupled with the target radiofrequency (RF) magnetic field, thereby forming RF-dressed states. Unlike pulsed dynamical-decoupling schemes, this approach does not require high-power microwave pulse sequences. The key feature of our method is that the detectable frequency can be tuned by controlling the bias magnetic field strength. Using this methodology, we have experimentally achieved the frequency-tunable detection of AC magnetic fields from 14.4-632 MHz. The sensitivity was 0.558 uT/Hz^(0.5) at 14.4 MHz and 24.4 uT/Hz^(0.5) at 632 MHz. We characterized the field-dependent ODMR contrast and linewidth, and found that their degradation at large transverse fields is a important factor limiting the high-frequency sensitivity. These results establish a simple CW-ODMR approach for widely tunable RF magnetic-field sensing without high-power pulsed spin control.

Abstract: We study how a neural network reorganizes its learned function as training proceeds and as previously unseen parity data accumulate. The trained model is itself a Boolean function on binary strings of finite length. We then evaluate it on all data, inside and outside the training set, and read off its complete sign table. Its $\pm1$ entries are the amplitudes of a normalized state, dubbed the function-state, whose inverse subsystem purity $K_f=1/\textrm{Tr}(ρ_A^2)$ is an effective Schmidt rank. We find that small data additions broaden the Schmidt spectrum, whereas larger additions reconcentrate it in the target mode. The former is called differentiation because the model distinguishes inputs more sharply. The latter is called target-selective recoherence, where the model settles on the single rule shared by the added data. As the dataset grows, the connectivity of the constraint graph defined by the training set increases monotonically, whereas the learned inverse purity changes nonmonotonically. On a fixed connected dataset, as learning proceeds, the Schmidt spectrum begins to reorganize, followed by gains in target alignment and later by improvement in predictive accuracy. This ordering distinguishes three aspects of learning: constraint identifiability, function-state organization, and predictive accuracy. At fixed data size, a function that has already been learned can re-differentiate, an event we call retreat, without changing graph connectivity. Recovery reconcentrates the Schmidt spectrum in a similar target-selective pattern, without exact recurrence of the Boolean state. Thus the percolation of data constraints fixes the identifiable support, while the reduced state produced by training continues to reorganize even after that support stops changing.

</details>

Abstract: The Zak phase provides a fundamental geometric characterization of one-dimensional band structures and, when quantized by symmetry, serves as a bulk indicator of polarization and boundary phenomena. Conventionally, its exact quantization is tied to a global symmetry of the Bloch Hamiltonian, such as inversion or reflection symmetry. Here, we demonstrate a distinct mechanism by which the Zak phase remains exactly quantized even when the corresponding protecting symmetry is explicitly broken at the level of the full Hamiltonian. The mechanism is based on local support symmetry and destructive interference: symmetry-breaking degrees of freedom are coupled to a topological parent system in such a way that the relevant Bloch states cannot leak into them. The preserved states therefore retain their parent wave functions and inherit their quantized Zak phase exactly, despite the absence of the protecting global symmetry. We establish this mechanism for general one-dimensional insulators and demonstrate it in an inversion-broken minimal model and a reflection-broken armchair nanoribbon, where a quantized $π$ Zak phase and associated boundary-localized states persist. We further show that perturbations violating the exact destructive-interference condition continuously dequantize the Zak phase, while a finite regime with a near-$π$ Zak phase and boundary response can survive. Our results establish wave-function support, rather than global symmetry alone, as a mechanism for protecting quantized quantum geometry in one-dimensional systems.

Abstract: Solving combinatorial optimization problems on near-term quantum computers mainly relies on their quadratic unconstrained binary formulation. Lately, binary polynomial formulations start to take the center stage, having a better scaling with respect to the number of used qubits. However, while quadratic forms are straightforward to implement on quantum circuits, polynomial forms of degree $d$ incur in circuit depth overheads, due to the lack of $d$-qubit gates, for $d\geq 3$, on current hardware. In this paper, we study the problem of designing the shallowest quantum circuit for a given polynomial form. We formulate it as a classical binary linear problem and we prove the \textsf{NP}-completeness of a variant of it. On the algorithmic side, we offer a scalable and performant heuristic method to tackle it, Tempura, as well as faster binary linear problem formulations, which offer computational feasibility while sacrificing optimality. Our performance evaluation shows that the algorithms yield far shorter circuits than the state-of-the-art approaches, and Tempura is mostly comparable with them in terms of runtime.

We perform this comparison by defining the secure communication rate (SCR) as the rate jointly supported by secret-key generation and classical transport, and using it to optimize a shared discrete-modulation CV-QKD link. The model combines a binomial 64-QAM security bound with a trusted receiver, protocol traffic, spectral leakage - optical power leaking into the quantum receive band - and Raman scattering. At 25 km, the asymptotic model gives an optimized SCR of 12.079 Mbps, compared with 12.095 Mbps for a separated-link reference, while reducing the transmitter and fiber counts from two to one each in the modeled direction. Increasing channel separation suppresses spectral leakage, but the resulting SCR gain diminishes once receiver bandwidth limits the rate. Under the adopted receiver-noise law, increasing the bandwidth budget from 350 to 750 MHz raises the projected 25-km SCR to 24.535 Mbps despite higher trusted electronic noise. For the studied metropolitan link, sharing saves optical resources with little rate loss, while receiver-bandwidth improvements provide greater benefit than further spectral separation. Together, these results suggest an integration path: share the transmitter and fiber, limit unwanted classical power in the quantum receive band, and increase usable receiver bandwidth where the noise budget permits.

Abstract: Quantum reservoir computing and its special case, quantum extreme learning, are powerful tools for predicting classical discrete time-series with quantum devices. Forecasting continuous signals, however, requires careful discretization as oversampling artificially boosts short-term accuracy but fails over realistic intervals, while undersampling captures too few features and yields poor predictions. Here, we experimentally generate four continuous time-series signals from a chaotic electronic circuit for forecasting with quantum extreme learning. Since simplified circuit models do not fully capture real behaviour, it is unclear whether algorithms that predict simulated time-series also work for experimental signals. We address discretization via two approaches: (i) sampling local peaks/troughs; and (ii) uniform sampling at the autocorrelation timescale. The former captures resonant behaviour, while the latter fully captures chaotic features as well as allowing the reconstruction of chaotic attractors. Our algorithm achieves high forecasting accuracy for both simulated and experimental signals, demonstrating its power for continuous signal processing on near-term quantum simulators, and remains robust against moderate decoherence.

Abstract: Josephson junctions are the central nonlinear element of superconducting qubits, yet their standard description is reached through a chain of approximations. We propose a purely quantum-mechanical model of the junction, formulated directly in Hilbert space: a charged particle moves on a one-dimensional loop, and the junction is a point at which the wave function obeys self-adjoint boundary conditions, labelled by a coupling strength $λ$, which controls the transparency of the junction, and a phase jump $δ$, which plays the role of the superconducting phase difference. For a loop with a single junction we solve the spectral problem and show that the ground-state momentum expectation, identified with the supercurrent, factorises exactly as $\langle\hat{p}_0\rangle = I_c(δ,λ)\sinδ$. For opaque junctions $I_c$ becomes independent of $δ$ and the first Josephson relation $I=I_c\sinδ$ is recovered; for strongly attractive coupling, where the ground state is bound to the junction, the $\sinδ$ law is also recovered, but with an exponentially suppressed amplitude. For more transparent junctions, however, the model \emph{predicts} deviations from the pure $\sinδ$ law: $I_c$ acquires a dependence on $δ$ already at order $λ^{-2}$, compatible with the higher Josephson harmonics recently found necessary to describe superconducting qubit spectra, and a fully transparent contact yields a current linear in $δ$. When the phase varies in time, a gauge transformation shows that the voltage drop across the junction is proportional to $\dotδ$, so that the second Josephson relation emerges without being imposed. The boundary-condition approach thus reproduces both Josephson relations from first principles and predicts corrections to them, offering a route towards higher-fidelity models of superconducting qubits.

</details>

Abstract: We derive approximation rates for Sobolev functions defined on the complex unit sphere and complex projective space, representing quantum states, and on the special and projective unitary groups, representing quantum unitary operations. Combining spectral truncation with finite-depth quantum-circuit approximation, invoking the Solovay--Kitaev theorem, we establish quantitative rates that separate the spectral truncation error from the circuit approximation error.

Abstract: A useful thermodynamic model predicts what a device will do without tracking its full microscopic state. For a finite quantum working medium, the difficult question is which quantities should actually be measured. We develop a task-based method that keeps exact energy accounting separate from calibrated macroscopic prediction. Operator geometry identifies what a measured record determines and bounds what remains uncertain. We then compare direct measurements of dynamical observables with earlier measurements of the same coarse record, selecting the representation together with its measurement cost. A Bose--Hubbard example yields a compact response table tested over a continuous operating region and on unseen microscopic trajectories. A driven Ising chain shows why history is not always the cheaper choice. The result is a way to turn detailed quantum dynamics into a small, reusable, and explicitly qualified thermodynamic description.

Abstract: We study correlation-based entanglement evaluation in rank-2 mixed states of two- and multi-qubit systems. The relevant two-dimensional subspace is treated as a logical qubit embedded in the physical Hilbert space, and factorizable realizations of its logical Pauli operators are classified. The relation between logical and physical Bloch vectors admits a geometric interpretation as a transversely deformed Bloch sphere, leading to closed-form expressions for two-qubit concurrence and generalized concurrence across $N$-qubit bipartitions. The entanglement scaling factor is obtained directly from complementary-subsystem correlations, without prior calibration of state-preparation parameters. Noisy simulations using the IBM Quantum FakeKingston backend model, with readout-error mitigation and zero-noise extrapolation, confirm the analytical results up to $N=10$ qubits. The protocol therefore enables entanglement estimation from only a small number of measurement settings for this class of rank-2 logical states.

We thus show that the B-XZZX pattern found by EBBI can be systematically scaled to larger code distances. Our work represents a first step towards scalable CD optimization for general QEC codes.

Abstract: Resource constraints and imperfect quantum operations remain major challenges for quantum machine learning (QML) on noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) devices, particularly for fault-diagnosis tasks involving imbalanced and heterogeneous data. This work proposes QTN-RQNN, a Quantum Tensor Network-Based Recurrent Quantum Neural Network that integrates tensor-network-inspired nearest-neighbor interactions with recurrent quantum processing. The model sequentially encodes input features while propagating quantum memory across recurrent steps and incorporates QSMOTE for class balancing. QTN-RQNN is evaluated on four heterogeneous fault-diagnosis datasets SPID, CWRUBD, EFDD, and SFDD and compared with QNN, QTN-QNN, and RQNN baselines through classification, ablation, multi-seed, convergence, and quantum-resource analyses. Results show dataset-dependent predictive behavior, indicating that increased quantum architectural complexity does not universally improve classification. On SPID, QTN-RQNN improves the RQNN F1-score from 0.3077 to 0.4516 and ROC-AUC from 0.4485 to 0.6970, while the remaining datasets exhibit different performance-resource trade-offs and sensitivity to recurrent timesteps and circuit depth. Across all datasets, QTN-RQNN reduces the two-qubit-gate count from 32 to 24 per sample relative to RQNN, corresponding to a 25% reduction without increasing the four-qubit requirement; the same relative reduction is obtained for QTN-QNN over QNN. These findings establish QTN-RQNN as a resource-conscious recurrent quantum architecture and highlight the need to jointly consider predictive performance and circuit-resource requirements for near-term quantum fault diagnosis.

Abstract: Dissipative state preparation engineers quantum many-body states as steady states of an open-system dynamics. Here, we establish an exact locality-preparation time relation for quadratic fermionic systems and develop a construction that optimizes the locality of the dissipative dynamics while preserving the target state. At criticality, suppressing the algebraic tails of the jump operators necessarily forces the preparation time to grow algebraically with system size. By contrast, both trivial and topological gapped phases admit exponentially localized dissipation with a finite preparation time in the thermodynamic limit. Our results identify when fast dissipative preparation is compatible with local control and when criticality imposes an unavoidable dynamical cost.

Abstract: Scaling trapped-ion processors by adding ions remains challenging. The architecture natively supports both phase-space and sideband instruction sets and is implemented in the open-source framework HybridLane. For every finite value of the constraint, their common value is the coherent information optimized over Fock-diagonal input states satisfying the constraint. These chain rules give an upper bound on the rates achievable by adaptive protocols whose mean input photon number is constrained on average over all channel uses. Our CUDA implementation combines disordered-memory retries, sliding windows, correlation-aware graphs, and layered scheduling. Circuit-level simulations test fixed-basis memory and complete logical Pauli-frame recovery. On the [[144,12,12]] bivariate bicycle code at physical error rate p = 0.002, parallel BP-SF reduces 99.9th-percentile call latency by a factor of 13.15 relative to an eight-chain Relay-BP GPU baseline on 100,000 matched samples for which every tested method's initial BP stage fails within 80 iterations. Windowed BP-SF maintains a flat logical error rate and nearly constant per-round compute time through 512 rounds, reaching a 17.1-fold speedup over monolithic decoding. In 512-round full-frame streaming decoding with 2.97-ms syndrome arrivals, layered BP-SF on a transformed joint graph with 20 retries reduces 99.9th-percentile correction-release age from 166.29 to 36.25 ms relative to the joint graph with flooding updates and 100 retries. These results connect parallel BP post-processing to lower decoding costs and earlier correction release in streaming quantum memory.

Abstract: Two-block group-algebra codes are among the simplest quantum LDPC codes, and many of them are translation invariant on a twisted torus, which suits neutral-atom arrays and other platforms with movable qubits. We ask how far they go at small sizes when searched systematically across all groups. To tackle this question, we use a series of algebraic constraints and screens to design a search pipeline capable of enumerating broad and complete swathes of two-block codes on a laptop. With it we census about $10^8$ two-block codes with up to $320$ qubits at check weights five to nine. Several of these censuses are exhaustive, and support complete statements; for instance, the Gross code is, up to equivalence, the only abelian weight-six two-block code with $n \le 200$ to reach $kd^2/n=12$, and the known $[[64,18,8]]$ is the best two-block code by $kd^2/n$ across weights five to nine with $n \le 94$. The censuses are also large enough to give insights into where good codes live - they concentrate in a handful of groups; abelian groups give the best codes at small $n$ and non-abelian groups at large $n$, with the crossover appearing to move out as the check weight grows; and high quality codes become $100$ to $1000$ times rarer per draw from weight six to weights eight and nine. Using the group algebra and the census data to steer a statistical search finds four to fifteen times as many good codes as uniform draws in equal time. Among the codes found are $[[102,20,10]]$ and $[[120,24,10]]$ at check weight nine, which we simulate in noisy syndrome-extraction circuits, and $[[288,16,16]]$, the best weight-six two-block code with $n \le 300$. At check weights seven to nine, our best codes exceed the best published $kd^2/n$ with exact distance at the same check weight in most length bands up to $320$ qubits. All codes are available in a public database.

</details>

Abstract: Fermionic Gaussian states are completely characterized by their two-point correlations, making the covariance matrix a natural reference for quantifying non-Gaussian structure. Motivated by fermionic antiflatness, we study its extremal form for pure number-conserving states and ask which states can be maximally antiflat and how they can be constructed. We show that, at fixed particle number, maximal first antiflatness is equivalent to a completely flat one-body reduced density matrix, and we obtain a complete existence classification together with explicit block and cyclic-orbit constructions of all allowed sectors. We then investigate the structure that appears beyond the covariance level. Maximally antiflat states with identical one-body data can exhibit distinct connected four-point correlations, which we characterize through the four-point Majorana Wick error and, equivalently, the connected two-body reduced density matrix cumulant. We show that this quartic structure is necessarily nonzero and formulate its extremization as a two-body reduced density matrix (2RDM) purity problem. Combining this framework with a Hilbert--Schmidt estimate of Christiansen for truncated two-body correlations, we obtain an upper bound on the connected quartic structure in terms of first antiflatness. Our results establish both a complete characterization of maximal fermionic antiflatness at fixed particle number and a constrained hierarchy linking covariance-level flatness to higher-order non-Gaussian correlations.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 1) 证明了n顶点锦标赛图中寻找最大出度顶点需要Θ(n^{3/2})量子查询，该界即使仅计算最大出度值也成立，解决了FSTTCS'23中提出的猜想；2) 直接证明了n顶点无向图中寻找最大度顶点需要Θ(n^{3/2})量子查询，这一界限隐含在Apers和Lee的工作中，现提供了独立证明；3) 对Maximum (k,n-k)-Cut问题，给出了所有常数k≥1情况下O(n^{2-1/(k+1)})查询复杂度的量子算法；4) 通过归约技术，对k≥3的情况建立了指数严格大于3/2的无条件下界，证明这些问题在量子复杂度上严格难于最大度计算；在k-Graph Collision猜想成立的条件下，归约产生的下界与算法上界匹配。

Conclusion: 本文系统地刻画了多个基本图问题的量子查询复杂度。通过统一的证明技术确立了最大出度和最大度问题的Θ(n^{3/2})紧确界，发展了Maximum (k,n-k)-Cut问题的量子算法并建立了相应的下界理论。研究结果揭示了图问题量子复杂度的精细层级结构：某些问题（如k≥3的Maximum (k,n-k)-Cut）在量子模型下严格难于最大度计算问题。通过k-Graph Collision问题的归约框架和相关猜想，本文为理解图问题量子复杂度的本质难度提供了新工具和理论基础，推进了量子算法理论在图算法领域的深入发展。

Abstract: We study the quantum query complexity of several basic graph problems. 1) We prove a tight $Θ(n^{3/2})$ bound for finding a maximum-out-degree vertex in an $n$-vertex tournament, resolving a conjecture of Mande, Paraashar, and Saurabh [FSTTCS'23]. 2) Using the same proof idea, we give a direct proof that finding a maximum-degree vertex in an $n$-vertex undirected graph requires $Θ(n^{3/2})$ quantum queries, a bound implicit in the work of Apers and Lee [CCC'21]. Both lower bounds above hold even for computing only the maximum degree or maximum out-degree. 3) We also study Maximum $(k,n-k)$-Cut, which asks for the largest cut with one side of size $k$. For every constant $k\geq 1$, we give an $O(n^{2-1/(k+1)})$-query quantum algorithm. 4) We establish a reduction to Maximum $(k,n-k)$-Cut involving $k$-Graph Collision, which asks whether the marked vertices of a known graph contain a $k$-clique. For $k\geq 3$, this yields unconditional lower bounds with exponents strictly larger than $3/2$, showing that this problem is strictly harder than computing maximum degree. Additionally, under a conjecture we propose for $k$-Graph Collision, the reduction yields a lower bound matching our upper bound above.

Conclusion: 成功开发了基于JAX的可扩展SQD基态求解器,该求解器能够处理高达2^30维的子空间对角化问题,在GPU内存效率和多GPU强扩展性方面均表现出色。这为在高性能计算平台上进行大规模量子多体系统的基态计算提供了有效工具,为SQD方法的实际应用奠定了技术基础,并为未来更大规模的量子计算问题求解提供了可能性。

Abstract: A scalable ground-state solver to be used in sample-based quantum diagonalization (SQD) is presented. The solver is built on JAX API, and is intended to be run on GPU platforms and possibly many-GPU high-performance computing facilities. It is capable of subspace diagonalization in up to $2^{30}$-dimensional subspace, with possible upgrades to enable even larger-scale operations. The two main components, a general-purpose eigensolver and an SQD-specific matrix-vector operation function, are described in detail. Efficiency of the eigensolver with respect to the GPU memory is demonstrated using a simple physics model. Furthermore, good strong scaling of the eigensolver in many-GPU environment is observed.

Abstract: Quantum mechanics seems to be an incredibly delicate theory. Slight alterations to nearly any part of its structure can lead to huge qualitative differences and wildly unphysical predictions. Here, we explore the many-body consequences of replacing the Born rule $p(m)\propto\|P_m|ψ\rangle|^2$ by a warped rule $p_b(m)\propto\|P_m|ψ\rangle\|^{2b}$. Specifically, in systems with conserved particle number, evolution punctuated by repeated local particle observations acquires a probabilistic bias over measurement histories with an effect controlled by the sign of $b-1$. For $b>1$, this bias generically drives the gas to collect into nearly frozen configurations. Similar to the action of Maxwell's demon, the simple act of observation seems to extract entropy from a closed system. For $b<1$, the bias is reversed, generically scouring out long-wavelength fluctuations to produce a hyperuniform gas. We study this effect in a one-dimensional quantum gas, where these two phases are derived analytically for a particular measurement scheme and corroborated numerically for more general procedures with discrete-time projective-measurement circuit models using matrix product state simulations. We additionally discuss how this bias manifests in a common technique that often employs this warped rule, the ``replica trick,'' implying qualitative differences between finite-replica and replica-limit calculations.

Abstract: Scattering of quasiparticles provides direct access to the interactions that govern strongly correlated systems. In this work, we realize the quasiparticle scattering experiment of the Fermi-Hubbard model on a digital quantum processor by colliding a doublon and a holon in the half-filled chain of 21 sites encoded on 42 qubits of the ibm_boston processor. For the first time, we measure the transmission probability of quasiparticles of the Hubbard model on quantum hardware, which agrees with the Bethe-ansatz scattering matrix without free parameters. Beyond the scattering probabilities, the collision generates entanglement by superposing the transmitted and reflected components, which we infer from the particle-number fluctuation. In sharp contrast, a control collision between two doublons shows neither the interaction dependence nor the number fluctuation, as expected for indistinguishable particles. Our work demonstrates that the ability to create quasiparticles of a chosen flavor on demand and program their motion and interactions makes a digital quantum processor a new experimental toolset for probing the entanglement structure generated by quasiparticle scattering.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of charged test particles around a Letelier black hole, i.e. a black hole surrounded by a cloud of strings (CoS), in Kalb-Ramond (KR) gravity, immersed in an external, asymptotically uniform magnetic field aligned with the symmetry axis. The geometry depends on the CoS parameter and on the Lorentz-violating KR parameter only through a single combination of the two, so that the two effects can compensate each other and the Schwarzschild metric is recovered when both parameters are equal. Because the spacetime is not Ricci flat, the Wald potential is not a solution of the Maxwell equations; we show that the regular source-free magnetic test field is given in closed form by a Gauss hypergeometric function of the radial coordinate, which reduces to the Wald solution in the Schwarzschild limit. Using this exact field we derive the equations of motion, the effective potential, the circular orbits, the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO), representative escaping, captured and curled trajectories, and the orbital, radial and vertical epicyclic frequencies in closed form; all frequency expressions are cross-checked against direct numerical integration of the full equations of motion. Within the relativistic precession model, we perform a Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) analysis of the twin-peak high-frequency quasi-periodic oscillations (QPOs) of GRO J1655-40, XTE J1550-564 and GRS 1915+105 for four nested models, complemented by nested sampling to compute Bayesian evidences and to weight the two (Larmor and anti-Larmor) magnetic modes of the posterior. The Schwarzschild model is excluded for GRO J1655-40 and XTE J1550-564, with a difference in the Akaike information criterion of about 110.

Conclusion: 本研究解决了Einstein-aether理论中渐近反德西特黑洞热力学的关键难题,建立了包含以太场渐近倾斜效应的完整第一定律。研究揭示了物理温度通常无法从黑洞质量中面积变化项的系数直接推断的深层原因,即普适视界处的热量不仅包含几何贡献还包含以太场的能量流,这为具有优选时间方向理论中的黑洞热力学提供了新的理解框架。

Abstract: Universal horizons trap even arbitrarily fast signals, providing a test of black-hole thermodynamics in theories with a preferred time direction. In Einstein--aether theory, this direction is defined by a dynamical field, the aether, that can remain non-aligned relative to stationary time at infinity. In particular, for asymptotically anti-de Sitter black holes, it was so far impossible to recover a first law of black-hole mechanics when such asymptotic tilt of the aether is present. Here, we resolve this issue for an exact family of static, spherically symmetric solutions. Using the covariant phase-space formalism, we identify the asymptotic tilt and the decaying part of the aether field as a boundary source and its conjugate response. Allowing both to vary independently obstructs the definition of an energy that generates time translations. Fixing either quantity, or imposing a relation between them, restores such an energy, while externally varying the boundary data introduces work into the first law. The energies obtained by fixing the source or its response are related by a Legendre transformation. At the universal horizon, heat includes both the metric area contribution and an irreducible aether energy flux. The resulting balance separates work at infinity from horizon heat and explains why the physical temperature cannot generally be inferred from the coefficient of an area variation in the black-hole mass.

Abstract: We study the reheating epoch and total bounce-to-present expansion in modified loop quantum cosmology using current observational determinations of the scalar amplitude and spectral index. We consider the power-law plateau potential, whose normalization is determined independently from the observed values of the scalar amplitude and spectral index. We find that, for the kinetic-energy-dominated initial conditions, the quantum-bounce regime is remarkably insensitive to this normalization, while the subsequent inflationary and reheating evolution shows a more appreciable dependence. In an axial Schwarzschild example at a fixed matching plane, accounting for the predicted linear-delay difference reduces the maximum relative cavity-factor difference between two cutoffs from about $21\%$ to $0.11\%$ on the sampled frequency band. Abstract: We present what are, to our knowledge, the first 3D numerical-relativity evolutions of wormhole binaries: pairs of massive Ellis-Bronnikov drainholes held open by a ghost scalar field. A throat survives at most a few orbital periods for any plausible perturbation, and the negative-energy dipole would pump rather than drain the orbit, so these binaries have no inspiral.

Abstract: Understanding the gravitational interaction is one of the great scientific endeavors of the 21st century. The Nobel Prize-winning discovery of gravitational waves in 2015, and subsequent detections by the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA Collaboration, have marked a revolutionary step forward in physics and astrophysics by opening an entirely new window for exploring the Universe. Future upgrades to these detectors, along with upcoming facilities such as LISA, the Einstein Telescope, and radio and optical instruments, promise other breakthrough discoveries that the next generation of scientists will need to interpret. The School on Gravity (22--26 June 2026, Copenhagen) aimed to introduce junior scientists--graduate students and advanced undergraduates with a background in general relativity--to a wide range of foundational topics in the exciting new field of gravitational-wave physics. Topics spanned from the theory of motion, to new challenges introduced by quantum physics, to the astrophysics of black holes and source modeling for gravitational-wave detectors. The School on Gravity took place at the legendary Auditorium A of the Niels Bohr Institute, inviting students and lecturers to challenge current paradigms in gravitational physics in the spirit of the quantum revolution that unfolded in this very room. These lecture notes aim at opening up those new ideas presented in Auditorium A to the rest of the scientific community.

Abstract: Simulations of the evolution of matter on static metric backgrounds allow us to estimate backreaction effects like dynamical friction at first order in the matter density. We verify that our implementation reproduces previous results quantifying dynamical friction in wave-like dark matter in the appropriate regimes. We discuss the subtleties of transforming diagnostic observables between frames in a 3+1 evolution and develop a general framework for this process.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是探讨引力理论中一个基本问题：线性化度规自旋-2场的平移Noether流能否构造出严格规范不变的局域表达式。这涉及引力理论中能量-动量守恒流的定义及其与规范对称性的关系，是理解引力能量定义和表面电荷代数的关键问题。通过与Maxwell理论、自由p-形式场和Yang-Mills理论等规范理论对比，试图阐明自旋-2场的特殊性质。

The translation current nevertheless defines a nonzero conserved-current class. Whereas for the RS warped geometry model, a tiny deviation in the radius of the extra dimension produces a large change in the same, resulting a sharp increment. Abstract: We study open strings in a constant magnetic field from the viewpoint of universal canonical variables. Abstract: Holographic complexity is expected to capture the linear growth of black hole interiors, with quantum corrections ultimately leading to a late-time saturation. The late-time plateau is a direct result of the spectral level repulsion, a generic feature of quantum chaotic systems, thereby providing a universal mechanism for the saturation of holographic complexity. We also develop a complementary operator interpretation, in which Euclidean wormhole corrections render the semiclassical basis of fixed-complexity states overcomplete. Constructing a complete orthonormal basis then yields well-defined holographic complexity operators that reproduce the same universal time evolution. Abstract: We analytically study the peak statistics of an amplitude ensemble to identify the local mechanism responsible for peak repulsion. Abstract: We discuss General Relativity and some of its modifications as effective field theories of the breaking of the affine general linear group using the coset approach and the Maurer-Cartan form. Since this cancellation does not occur in the CF model, it could provide a signature of the topological origin of the photon mass in $(2+1)D$. Finally, the PEC-PMC2 case yields a non-vanishing Casimir force and reproduces the known result for a perfectly conducting wire facing an infinitely permeable one. Interestingly enough, we show that for PEC-PMC2, the Casimir force in the MCS theory is smaller than the one of the CF model for any $L,μ>0$. Our results thus show that, depending on the boundary conditions, the Casimir effect could probe not only the photon mass but also reveal signatures of its origin.

Abstract: We investigate holographic Krylov complexity in bottom-up holographic QCD models describing the confined phase, using the recently proposed correspondence between the growth rate of Krylov complexity and the proper radial momentum of probes, such as particles and strings, falling in the bulk spacetime. As representative confining backgrounds, we consider two horizonless geometries, namely the dilaton-deformed AdS-soliton and the deformed thermal-AdS spacetime, both arising as solutions of an Einstein-dilaton gravity system. We find that the Krylov complexity associated with a massive particle exhibits qualitatively distinct behavior in these two backgrounds when analyzed in the Einstein frame. In the deformed AdS-soliton geometry, the complexity undergoes periodic oscillations, with both the oscillation period and amplitude depending on the parameters of the model. In contrast, in the deformed thermal-AdS geometry, the complexity grows exponentially at late times and does not display oscillatory behavior. We then investigate string dynamics in the string frame and find that the Krylov complexity exhibits oscillatory behavior in both confining backgrounds. This behavior is traced to the emergence of an additional turning point in the deep infrared of the string-frame geometry. Motivated by this observation, we further analyze massive-particle dynamics in the string frame, where we find qualitatively similar behavior in the two confining backgrounds. Our results demonstrate that the Krylov complexity of confined holographic systems depends sensitively on the choice of probe and frame, while also revealing a closer correspondence between the two confining geometries in the string-frame description.

Abstract: The $SU(2)$ Skyrme model admits exact solutions when coupled to General Relativity in four spacetime dimensions. Here, we demonstrate that relevant gravitational configurations, such as black holes, wormholes, and cosmologies, are robust under the extension to $SU(N)$ with an arbitrary number of flavors. We show that key physical features, such as the thermodynamic properties, the baryon number of the configurations, and the geometry, are crucially determined by the number of flavors as well as the coupling constants of the theory. In particular: first, we prove that an increase in the matter content favors the local thermal stability of spherically symmetric black holes. Second, for cosmological solutions, we demonstrate analytically that as the hadronic matter content increases, the scale factor grows more slowly due to enhanced gravitational attraction, while the expansion rate (measured by the Hubble parameter) increases as a consequence of the higher energy density. Third, we find that the throat of traversable wormhole solutions can be enlarged by increasing the number of flavors, since the energy density required to keep the throat open does not grow indefinitely but rather saturates at a finite value.

Motivation: 广义相对论中黑弦存在Gregory-Laflamme不稳定性,而爱因斯坦-高斯-博内引力作为高维引力理论的重要修正,其对黑弦不稳定性的影响及非线性演化行为尚未充分理解。特别需要研究高斯-博内耦合如何改变不稳定性增长率,以及在强耦合区域可能出现的演化崩溃问题。

Method: 1) 对五维爱因斯坦-高斯-博内理论中的均匀黑弦进行时间相关微扰分析,计算不同耦合常数下的增长率;2) 在渐近平直方向限制为球对称动力学时,分析运动方程的双曲性由有效度规决定;3) 应用柯西演化崩溃判据检验均匀黑弦解的正则性;4) 进行Gregory-Laflamme不稳定性的全非线性数值模拟验证理论预测。

Result: 正高斯-博内耦合下黑弦比广义相对论更不稳定,表现为更大的增长率和更短波长的不稳定模式。对于任意正高斯-博内耦合,存在开集的正则解(包括最小厚度解),其有效度规在视界附近退化,导致这些解无法从弱耦合初始数据通过柯西演化达到。非线性模拟证实演化会因Tricomi型或Keldysh型双曲性丧失而终止,具体类型取决于高斯-博内耦合的符号。

Conclusion: 爱因斯坦-高斯-博内引力中的黑弦在正耦合情况下表现出增强的Gregory-Laflamme不稳定性。关键发现是在强耦合区域,有效场论的有效度规会退化,导致双曲性丧失和柯西演化的根本性崩溃。这意味着某些正则黑弦解实际上无法通过物理演化过程达到,线性化近似在这些区域失效。该研究揭示了高阶曲率修正对黑洞时空稳定性和演化适定性的深刻影响。

Abstract: We complement and extend our recent study of the non-linear evolution of the Gregory-Laflamme instability of black strings in Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet gravity in five spacetime dimensions. First, we study time-dependent perturbations of uniform black strings in Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet theory and determine the growth rates as a function of the coupling. We find that for positive Gauss-Bonnet couplings, the black strings are more unstable than in general relativity in the sense that the growth rates are larger and shorter wavelengths are unstable. In the second part of the paper we show that the hyperbolicity of the equation of motion of Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet theory is determined by an effective metric when the dynamics is restricted to spherical symmetry in the asymptotically flat directions. When the effective field theory becomes strongly coupled, as it happens near the endpoint of the Gregory-Laflamme instability of the black string, this effective metric becomes degenerate and the Cauchy evolution breaks down. Applying this criterion to uniform black strings, we find that for any positive Gauss-Bonnet coupling there is an open set of regular solutions (including the one of minimal thickness) on which the effective metric degenerates in a neighbourhood of the horizon. Therefore, we argue that these solutions cannot be attained from weakly coupled initial data by Cauchy evolution and the solutions of the linearized equations around them cannot be interpreted as approximations to nearby non-linear evolutions. We provide further evidence for this picture with non-linear simulations of the Gregory-Laflamme instability. The non-linear simulations terminate through a loss of hyperbolicity of the Tricomi or Keldysh type, depending on the sign of the Gauss-Bonnet coupling.

</details>


### [97] [Dualities, symmetries and the elliptic genus of 2d (0,2) SCFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13389)
*Salomon Malka,Gabi Zafrir*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究二维(0,2)理论对偶中的全局对称性不匹配问题,发现椭圆亏格计算显示存在可以破缺这些对称性的边缘算符,这些算符非微扰地涌现,为解决对称性差异提供了潜在机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维(0,2)理论的对偶关系虽然通过了大部分一致性检验,但存在全局对称性不匹配的困惑。这种现象常见于从4维N=1理论对偶降维得到的情况,其中对称性在二维中涌现(通常来自4维的反常对称性)。尽管普遍认为这些对称性在二维理论中被破缺,但破缺机制往往不清楚,需要深入研究这一现象的物理机制。

Method: 通过计算和分析(0,2)理论的椭圆亏格(elliptic genus)来研究对称性破缺机制。椭圆亏格是二维超对称理论的重要不变量,可以揭示理论的算符谱信息。作者考察了该现象在不同情况下的多种表现形式,重点分析椭圆亏格计算中涌现的边缘算符的性质及其对全局对称性的影响。

Result: 在所研究的案例中,椭圆亏格显示出存在可以破缺这些全局对称性的边缘算符的迹象。值得注意的是,这些算符并非微扰地出现,而是从椭圆亏格计算中涌现出来的。这一发现引发了关于(0,2)椭圆亏格实际上计数哪些算符的深刻问题,表明椭圆亏格可能包含了超出传统微扰理论预期的信息。

Conclusion: 椭圆亏格中涌现的边缘算符为克服二维(0,2)理论对偶中的对称性差异提供了一种潜在机制,能够解释对偶理论之间全局对称性不匹配的现象。然而,作者也指出这一解释存在一些潜在的微妙之处和需要进一步澄清的问题,特别是关于椭圆亏格所计数的算符的确切物理含义。这为理解二维超对称理论的非微扰性质提供了新的视角。

Abstract: While naively simpler than its higher dimensional cousins, 2d physics still has its own share of puzzles. An example of this is instances of dualities of $(0,2)$ theories, passing all consistency checks save for a mismatch in global symmetry. This is common when said duality can be motivated by dimensional reduction of a duality of 4d $\mathcal{N}=1$ theories where said symmetry emerges in 2d, often from an anomalous 4d symmetry. It is commonly believed that these symmetries are broken in the 2d theory, though often the mechanism for such breaking is not understood. We examine various appearances of this phenomenon and show that in these cases the elliptic genus shows signs of the presence of marginal operators that can break these symmetries. Interestingly, these operators do not appear perturbatively but emerge from the calculation, raising the question of what operators the $(0,2)$ elliptic genus actually counts. This provides a potential mechanism to overcome the discrepancy in symmetry, though we point out some potential subtleties.

</details>


### [98] [$q$-deformed Weil-Petersson Volumes from the Spectral Edge of DSSYK and Supersymmetric Extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13899)
*Wasif Ahmed,Joydeep Naskar,Krishan Saraswat*

TL;DR: 本文研究了q-变形Weil-Petersson体积与双标度SYK模型的关系,证明这些体积可从双标度矩阵模型获得,并通过对N=1 DSSYK硬边缘应用相同方法构造了候选q-变形N=1超Weil-Petersson体积,展示了如何自然地将q-变形体积与各种DSSYK模型的谱边缘联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Do和Norbury的q-变形Weil-Petersson体积与Okuyama离散体积的关系尚不完全清楚,需要从矩阵模型角度理解这些体积的起源,并将q-变形体积的构造方法推广到超对称情况(特别是N=1情况),从而建立DSSYK模型谱边缘与q-变形体积之间的系统联系。

Result: 成功证明q-变形Weil-Petersson体积可从DSSYK软边缘的双标度矩阵模型获得。构造出的候选q-变形N=1超Weil-Petersson体积具有固定次数的多项式形式,在标度q→1极限下正确退化为N=1超Weil-Petersson体积。发现这些候选体积与Do和Norbury在arXiv:2510.12431中的N=1体积存在差异,差异来自在标度q→1极限下恰好消失的"无关"贡献。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了q-变形体积与DSSYK模型谱边缘之间的自然联系,提供了一种在超对称背景下构造q-变形体积的直观方法。通过矩阵模型的软/硬边缘缩放技术,统一了不同DSSYK模型的q-变形体积构造方案,并阐明了"无关"贡献在q→1极限中的作用,为理解拓扑递归与双标度极限之间的深层联系提供了新视角。

Abstract: The $q$-deformed Weil-Petersson volumes of Do and Norbury are the top-degree parts of Okuyama's discrete volumes, which are defined by topological recursion on the spectral curve of the double-scaled SYK (DSSYK) model. We show that the same q-deformed volumes can be obtained from a double-scaled matrix model whose disk density is found by zooming in on the soft edges of the DSSYK density. Applying the same procedure to the hard edge of $\mathcal{N}=1$ DSSYK, we obtain candidate $q$-deformed $\mathcal{N}=1$ super Weil-Petersson volumes. They are polynomials of fixed degree, and they reduce to the $\mathcal{N}=1$ super Weil-Petersson volumes in a scaled $q\to1$ limit. These candidate volumes differ from the $\mathcal{N}=1$ volumes of Do and Norbury in (arXiv:2510.12431) up to what we call "irrelevant" contributions which exactly vanish in the scaled $q\to 1$ limit. Together, these results demonstrate how q-deformed volumes can be naturally associated to the spectral edges of various DSSYK models and provides an intuitive way to construct them in supersymmetric contexts.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维N=1 class S超共形场论中轻算符谱的普适扇区，这些理论源于M5膜包裹在紧致双曲黎曼面上。通过大N极限下分析对偶AdS背景的Kaluza-Klein涨落，结合扭曲参数z依赖的广义G结构，将本征态组织为长超多重态和保护超多重态，揭示了随z变化的丰富谱结构模式。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 成功确定了四维N=1 class S超共形场论中轻算符谱的普适扇区。发现了丰富的谱结构模式，包括孤立的超多重态和无穷塔超多重态。这些结构随扭曲参数z的变化呈现出系统性的演化规律，所有结果均以解析形式给出，为理解该类理论的算符内容提供了完整的刻画。

Conclusion: 本研究通过AdS/CFT对偶关系系统地确定了四维N=1 class S超共形场论的轻算符谱普适扇区。解析结果揭示了扭曲参数z如何调控谱的结构，包括孤立多重态与无穷塔多重态的出现模式。这为理解M5膜理论的低能有效描述提供了重要的理论工具，也为进一步研究更一般的class S理论奠定了基础。

Abstract: We determine a universal sector of the spectrum of light operators in four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=1$ class $\mathcal{S}$ superconformal field theories arising from M5-branes wrapped on a compact hyperbolic Riemann surface $Σ_2$. We work at large $N$, analysing Kaluza--Klein fluctuations about the dual Bah--Beem--Bobev--Wecht anti-de Sitter backgrounds for general values of the twist parameter $z$. To obtain these spectra, we specialise recently derived mass operators to modes that are constant on $Σ_2$ by imposing a suitable $z$-dependent generalised $G$-structure. We then organise the resulting eigenstates into long and protected $\mathcal{N}=1$ supermultiplets with definite flavour quantum numbers. As $z$ varies, rich patterns emerge in the spectrum, including both isolated multiplets and infinite towers of multiplets, which we characterise analytically.

</details>

Motivation: 传统四费米子模型中存在强非多项式耦合项,这种非多项式性给理论处理带来困难。为了解决这一问题并研究可能的对称性破缺机制,需要发展新的理论框架来消除非多项式性,同时探索动力学洛伦兹对称性破缺的可能性。

Method: 引入两个辅助矢量场作为数学工具来消除原始模型中的非多项式耦合项;通过计算这些辅助矢量场的有效势来分析动力学洛伦兹对称性破缺的可能性;显式计算低能有效作用量,将其分解为麦克斯韦项和势能项的形式。

Abstract: We present a new four-fermion model with a strongly non-polynomial coupling. To eliminate these non-polynomialities, we introduce two auxiliary vector fields and show that the dynamical Lorentz symmetry breaking is possible in our theory, as revealed by the effective potential generated for these vector fields. We explicitly calculate the low-energy effective action for these fields, which is given by the sum of three Maxwell terms (one for each field and a mixed one) plus a potential term displaying nontrivial minima.

</details>

Conclusion: 本文成功构造并分析了G2定向折叠上具有完全模稳定化的尺度分离AdS₃模型，证明了几何奇点解消后模型在超引力近似下的可控性，澄清了定向折叠交线的几何本质，并通过膜对偶分析揭示了这些模型的重要物理特征，包括有限区间流方程解和低能膜模式退耦，为弦论紧致化和AdS/CFT对应研究提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We study scale-separated AdS$_3$ models with full moduli stabilization in massive IIA supergravity on G2 orientifolds. In addition to constructing new examples, we investigate the properties of these models once the singularities of the G2 toroidal orbifold are resolved. The blow-up moduli are shown to be stabilized using fluxes in a parametric regime that preserves scale separation and control under the supergravity approximation. We further show that the apparent orientifold intersections in the singular orbifold description do not exist on such resolved spaces. Finally, we analyze the brane dual picture of such models using flux backtracking. We highlight several interesting features from such analysis such as a finite interval solution of the resulting flow equations in some of the models and the desirable low-energy decoupling of brane modes.

</details>


### [102] [A discrete analogue of $\mathscr{N}=2$ super Weil-Petersson volumes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13667)
*Wasif Ahmed,Joydeep Naskar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从𝒩=2双标度SYK模型引入离散谱曲线,通过拓扑递归定义整数边界长度的拟多项式体积,并研究其局部𝒩=2边缘理论的有限q体积及其在模空间上的交理论解释。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在建立𝒩=2双标度SYK模型的离散谱曲线框架,并探索其拓扑递归性质与模空间几何之间的深层联系,特别是将有限q体积与超Weil-Petersson体积相关联,为理解量子引力和弦理论提供新的数学工具。
